# Graph Report - scripts  (2026-08-22)

## Corpus Check
- 1539 files · ~1,877,621 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.

## Summary
- 25165 nodes · 73882 edges · 1054 communities (1014 shown, 40 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 99% EXTRACTED · 1% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 493 edges (avg confidence: 0.61)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `95076aae`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
